Result: 在10多种配置的仿真和真实实验中展示了稳定对接、锁定和分离，以及有效控制性能。首次在真实世界中实现MARS的敏捷飞行和运输，峰值俯仰角达40度，平均位置误差0.0896米。

Conclusion: 提出的机械设计、虚拟四旋翼抽象和预测分配管道解决了MARS控制中的关键问题，实现了稳定对接分离和敏捷飞行控制，为模块化空中机器人系统提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: Modular Aerial Robot Systems (MARS) comprise multiple drone units with reconfigurable connected formations, providing high adaptability to diverse mission scenarios, fault conditions, and payload capacities. However, existing control algorithms for MARS rely on simplified quasi-static models and rule-based allocation, which generate discontinuous and unbounded motor commands. This leads to attitude error accumulation as the number of drone units scales, ultimately causing severe oscillations during docking, separation, and waypoint tracking. To address these limitations, we first design a compact mechanical system that enables passive docking, detection-free passive locking, and magnetic-assisted separation using a single micro servo. Second, we introduce a force-torque-equivalent and polytope-constraint virtual quadrotor that explicitly models feasible wrench sets. Together, these abstractions capture the full MARS dynamics and enable existing quadrotor controllers to be applied across different configurations. We further optimize the yaw angle that maximizes control authority to enhance agility. Third, building on this abstraction, we design a two-stage predictive-allocation pipeline: a constrained predictive tracker computes virtual inputs while respecting force/torque bounds, and a dynamic allocator maps these inputs to individual modules with balanced objectives to produce smooth, trackable motor commands. Simulations across over 10 configurations and real-world experiments demonstrate stable docking, locking, and separation, as well as effective control performance. To our knowledge, this is the first real-world demonstration of MARS achieving agile flight and transport with 40 deg peak pitch while maintaining an average position error of 0.0896 m. The video is available at: https://youtu.be/yqjccrIpz5o

</details>


### [110] [Simulation-Driven Evolutionary Motion Parameterization for Contact-Rich Granular Scooping with a Soft Conical Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05531)
*Yongliang Wang,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Tomoya Takahashi,Masashi Hamaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发基于物理的软锥形机器人手仿真模型，结合进化策略优化舀取轨迹，实现无需复杂传感的自适应舀取


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软机器人末端执行器能自适应容器形状提升舀取效率，但其柔顺性和复杂变形行为带来控制难题，限制了实际应用

Method: 提出基于物理的软锥形手仿真模型，准确模拟从平板到自适应锥形结构的变形行为，结合进化策略框架自动优化舀取轨迹

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证优化轨迹，展示强泛化能力，成功解决现有方法难以处理的挑战性任务

Conclusion: 基于物理的仿真与进化策略优化相结合的方法，有效解决了软机器人舀取任务的控制复杂性，拓展了其实际应用范围

Abstract: Tool-based scooping is vital in robot-assisted tasks, enabling interaction with objects of varying sizes, shapes, and material states. Recent studies have shown that flexible, reconfigurable soft robotic end-effectors can adapt their shape to maintain consistent contact with container surfaces during scooping, improving efficiency compared to rigid tools. These soft tools can adjust to varying container sizes and materials without requiring complex sensing or control. However, the inherent compliance and complex deformation behavior of soft robotics introduce significant control complexity that limits practical applications. To address this challenge, this paper presents the development of a physics-based simulation model of a deformable soft conical robotic hand that captures its passive reconfiguration dynamics and enables systematic trajectory optimization for scooping tasks. We propose a novel physics-based simulation approach that accurately models the soft tool's morphing behavior from flat sheets to adaptive conical structures, combined with an evolutionary strategy framework that automatically optimizes scooping trajectories without manual parameter tuning. We validate the optimized trajectories through both simulation and real-robot experiments. The results demonstrate strong generalization and successfully address a range of challenging tasks previously beyond the reach of existing approaches. Videos of our experiments are available online: https://sites.google.com/view/scoopsh

</details>


### [111] [Referring-Aware Visuomotor Policy Learning for Closed-Loop Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05544)
*Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin,Xin Wen,Yixiong Li,Yuyu Sun,Yulan Guo,Liang Lin,Ruimao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReV是一个用于机器人操作的视觉运动策略框架，通过耦合扩散头和轨迹引导策略，能够在仅使用原始专家演示训练的情况下，实时适应动态环境变化和稀疏参考点


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人操作中视觉运动策略学习的根本问题：如何在仅使用原始专家演示训练的情况下，增强模型在分布外执行错误或动态重规划轨迹时的鲁棒性

Method: 提出Referring-Aware Visuomotor Policy (ReV)闭环框架，采用耦合扩散头结构：全局扩散头生成全局一致但时间稀疏的动作锚点并定位参考点位置，局部扩散头基于当前时间位置自适应插值相邻锚点；仅通过对专家演示施加针对性扰动进行训练

Result: 无需额外数据或微调方案，在具有挑战性的仿真和真实世界任务中实现了更高的成功率

Conclusion: ReV框架能够有效适应意外情况，通过实时整合稀疏参考点实现闭环轨迹重规划，显著提升了机器人操作的鲁棒性和适应性

Abstract: This paper addresses a fundamental problem of visuomotor policy learning for robotic manipulation: how to enhance robustness in out-of-distribution execution errors or dynamically re-routing trajectories, where the model relies solely on the original expert demonstrations for training. We introduce the Referring-Aware Visuomotor Policy (ReV), a closed-loop framework that can adapt to unforeseen circumstances by instantly incorporating sparse referring points provided by a human or a high-level reasoning planner. Specifically, ReV leverages the coupled diffusion heads to preserve standard task execution patterns while seamlessly integrating sparse referring via a trajectory-steering strategy. Upon receiving a specific referring point, the global diffusion head firstly generates a sequence of globally consistent yet temporally sparse action anchors, while identifies the precise temporal position for the referring point within this sequence. Subsequently, the local diffusion head adaptively interpolates adjacent anchors based on the current temporal position for specific tasks. This closed-loop process repeats at every execution step, enabling real-time trajectory replanning in response to dynamic changes in the scene. In practice, rather than relying on elaborate annotations, ReV is trained only by applying targeted perturbations to expert demonstrations. Without any additional data or fine-tuning scheme, ReV achieve higher success rates across challenging simulated and real-world tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Control Architecture and experimental validation of a Novel Surgical Robotic Instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05610)
*Doina Pisla,Ionut Zima,Calin Vaida,Andrei Cailean,Marius Miclaus,Adrian Pisla,Andrei Caprariu,Vasile Bulbucan,Bogdan Gherman,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种4自由度柔性腹腔镜器械的控制架构，集成远端弯曲、独立远端头部旋转、轴旋转和夹持器，直径10mm兼容标准套管，通过Raspberry Pi 5和Motoron控制器实现驱动单元和SpaceMouse遥操作，验证了与ATHENA并联机器人的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术减少患者创伤并缩短恢复时间，但传统腹腔镜器械受限于运动范围有限。需要开发具有更大灵活性的器械来改善手术操作。

Method: 设计了4自由度柔性腹腔镜器械控制架构，包括远端弯曲、独立远端头部旋转、轴旋转和夹持器。采用Raspberry Pi 5和Motoron控制器实现驱动单元和SpaceMouse遥操作。推导并参数化了剪刀连杆机构的分析模型。通过CAD测量和OptiTrack运动捕捉验证模型精度。与ATHENA并联机器人集成，在模拟胰腺手术中验证。

Result: 器械直径10mm兼容标准套管。剪刀连杆模型预测的钳口开度与CAD测量结果一致（MAE 0.13°），与OptiTrack运动捕捉结果一致（MAE 1.43°）。成功与ATHENA并联机器人集成，在模拟胰腺手术中得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出的4自由度柔性腹腔镜器械控制架构有效解决了传统器械运动范围有限的问题，通过精确的机构建模和机器人集成验证了其在微创手术中的可行性。

Abstract: Minimally invasive surgery (MIS) reduces patient trauma and shortens recovery time; however, conventional laparoscopic instruments remain constrained by limited range of movements. This work presents the control architecture of a 4-DOF flexible laparoscopic instrument integrating distal bending, independent distal head rotation, shaft rotation, and a gripper, while maintaining a 10 mm diameter compatible with standard trocars. The actuation unit and SpaceMouse teleoperation are implemented on Raspberry Pi 5 with Motoron controllers. An analytical scissor-linkage model is derived and parameterized. The predicted jaw opening corresponds to CAD measurements (MAE 0.13{\textdegree}) and OptiTrack motion capture (MAE 1.43{\textdegree}). Integration with the ATHENA parallel robot is validated through a simulated pancreatic surgery procedure.

</details>


### [113] [Grounding Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Models Through Explicit Language-Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05614)
*Theodor Wulff,Federico Tavella,Rahul Singh Maharjan,Manith Adikari,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的训练框架，通过对比模型显式地对齐层次化视觉-语言-动作模型中的子任务描述与视觉观察和动作空间，使用离线偏好学习来提升多模态对齐，在LanguageTable数据集上取得了与全监督微调相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人透明度对于有效的人机协作至关重要，需要机器人的自然语言交流与其动作一致，并明确地基于任务和环境进行基础。现有的层次化视觉-语言-动作模型虽然能生成语言和低级动作，但在训练过程中没有考虑这些模态之间的显式对齐，这是一个关键的空白。

Method: 提出一个新颖的训练框架，显式地将层次化视觉-语言-动作模型的子任务描述与视觉观察和动作空间进行基础。使用对比模型评估生成语言与相应动作轨迹之间的对齐程度，该对比模型能够直接基于对齐程度对不同语言-轨迹对进行排序，从而通过离线偏好学习来优化层次化视觉-语言-动作模型的基础。

Result: 在LanguageTable数据集（人类语言标注轨迹的基准数据集）上应用该框架，提供了关于多模态基础表示的重要见解，同时建立了一个强大的基线，实现了与完全监督微调相当的性能，并最小化了昂贵数据标注的需求。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式地对齐语言和动作模态，解决了现有层次化视觉-语言-动作模型在多模态对齐方面的关键空白，为实现机器人透明度提供了有效的训练方法，同时减少了数据标注的成本。

Abstract: Achieving robot transparency is a critical step toward effective human-robot collaboration. To be transparent, a robot's natural language communication must be consistent with its actions and explicitly grounded in the task and environment. Existing hierarchical Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can generate language (e.g., through chain-of-thought) and low-level actions. However, current work does not consider explicit alignment between these modalities during training. To address this crucial gap, we propose a novel training framework that explicitly grounds hierarchical VLA sub-task descriptions with respect to the visual observation and action space. Our framework uses a contrastive model to assess the alignment between generated language and corresponding action trajectories. This contrastive model enables direct ranking of different language-trajectory pairs based on their alignment, allowing us to refine the grounding of our hierarchical VLA through offline preference learning. We apply our framework to the LanguageTable dataset, a benchmark dataset of human language-annotated trajectories, and provide critical insights into multimodal grounding representations, all while establishing a strong baseline that achieves performance comparable to fully supervised fine-tuning and minimizing the need for costly data annotations.

</details>


### [114] [Leaderless Collective Motion in Affine Formation Control over the Complex Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05648)
*Jesus Bautista,Enric Morella,Lili Wang,Hector Garcia de Marina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过修改拉普拉斯矩阵权重实现平面仿射编队集体机动的方法，生成时变仿射变换的集体运动，无需领导者且允许个体速度不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统领导者-跟随者策略限制了集体运动的多样性，需要一种无领导者方案使智能体能够维持不同且可能时变的速度，实现更广泛的集体运动类型。

Method: 通过修改用于实现静态编队的原始拉普拉斯矩阵权重，设计新的拉普拉斯矩阵，利用复数表示2D仿射编队的所有相关信息，提供控制集体运动的特征向量和特征值的解析解。

Result: 方法能够生成参考形状的所有线性组合（平移、旋转、缩放和剪切）的集体运动，通过最多20个智能体的仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 提出的无领导者方案扩展了仿射编队集体机动的能力，通过修改拉普拉斯矩阵权重和复数表示，实现了更灵活多样的集体运动控制。

Abstract: We propose a method for the collective maneuvering of affine formations in the plane by modifying the original weights of the Laplacian matrix used to achieve static formations of robot swarms. Specifically, the resulting collective motion is characterized as a time-varying affine transformation of a reference configuration, or shape. Unlike the traditional leader-follower strategy, our leaderless scheme allows agents to maintain distinct and possibly time-varying velocities, enabling a broader range of collective motions, including all the linear combinations of translations, rotations, scaling and shearing of a reference shape. Our analysis provides the analytic solution governing the resulting collective motion, explicitly designing the eigenvectors and eigenvalues that define this motion as a function of the modified weights in the new Laplacian matrix. To facilitate a more tractable analysis and design of affine formations in 2D, we propose the use of complex numbers to represent all relevant information. Simulations with up to 20 agents validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [115] [A1: A Fully Transparent Open-Source, Adaptive and Efficient Truncated Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05672)
*Kaidong Zhang,Jian Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Yu Sun,Shuoshuo Xue,Youpeng Wen,Xiaoyu Guo,Minghao Guo,Weijia Liufu,Liu Zihou,Kangyi Ji,Yangsong Zhang,Jiarun Zhu,Jingzhi Liu,Zihang Li,Ruiyi Chen,Meng Cao,Jingming Zhang,Shen Zhao,Xiaojun Chang,Feng Zheng,Ivan Laptev,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A1是一个开源VLA框架，通过自适应推理方案和层间截断流匹配技术，在保持高成功率的同时显著降低推理成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在实际部署中面临高成本问题：大规模VLM主干和迭代式动作头导致高延迟和高计算开销，难以在普通硬件上实现实时控制

Method: 采用预训练VLM提供隐式可供性先验，引入预算感知自适应推理方案，包括监控中间层动作一致性实现早期终止，以及提出层间截断流匹配技术减少去噪迭代次数

Result: 在仿真基准和真实机器人上达到最先进成功率，同时显著降低推理成本：流匹配推理延迟降低72%，主干计算减少76.6%，在RoboChallenge上以29.00%成功率超越基线模型

Conclusion: A1框架通过优化完整推理流水线，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，为低成本、高吞吐的机器人操作提供了可行的开源解决方案

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for open-world robot manipulation, but their practical deployment is often constrained by \emph{cost}: billion-scale VLM backbones and iterative diffusion/flow-based action heads incur high latency and compute, making real-time control expensive on commodity hardware. We present A1, a fully open-source and transparent VLA framework designed for low-cost, high-throughput inference without sacrificing manipulation success; Our approach leverages pretrained VLMs that provide implicit affordance priors for action generation. We release the full training stack (training code, data/data-processing pipeline, intermediate checkpoints, and evaluation scripts) to enable end-to-end reproducibility. Beyond optimizing the VLM alone, A1 targets the full inference pipeline by introducing a budget-aware adaptive inference scheme that jointly accelerates the backbone and the \emph{action head}. Specifically, we monitor action consistency across intermediate VLM layers to trigger early termination, and propose Inter-Layer Truncated Flow Matching that warm-starts denoising across layers, enabling accurate actions with substantially fewer effective denoising iterations. Across simulation benchmarks (LIBERO, VLABench) and real robots (Franka, AgiBot), A1 achieves state-of-the-art success rates while significantly reducing inference cost (e.g., up to 72% lower per-episode latency for flow-matching inference and up to 76.6% backbone computation reduction with minor performance degradation). On RoboChallenge, A1 achieves an average success rate of 29.00%, outperforming baselines including pi0(28.33%), X-VLA (21.33%), and RDT-1B (15.00%).

</details>


### [116] [Dynamic Control Allocation for Dual-Tilt UAV Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05677)
*Marcello Sorge,Federico Ciresola,Giulia Michieletto,Angelo Cenedese*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究六旋翼无人机平台的动态控制分配，重点关注双倾斜旋翼配置下的轨迹跟踪任务，提出考虑执行器饱和的分层控制结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双倾斜六旋翼无人机平台的控制分配问题，特别关注执行器饱和对控制性能的影响，以及如何利用系统冗余优化执行器状态。

Method: 采用分层控制结构：高层控制器生成轨迹跟踪所需的力和力矩（wrench），控制分配器确保执行器产生所需wrench。分配器对执行器施加一阶期望动力学，利用系统冗余优化目标函数，并显式建模执行器饱和。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了控制分配策略的有效性，分析了执行器饱和对控制性能的理论影响，并研究了通过目标函数中施加不对称形状来探索旋翼倾斜角的作用。

Conclusion: 论文提出的动态控制分配方法能够有效处理双倾斜六旋翼无人机的轨迹跟踪任务，显式考虑执行器饱和并提供理论分析，为冗余系统的控制分配提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: This paper focuses on dynamic control allocation for a hexarotor UAV platform, considering a trajectory tracking task as as case study. It is assumed that the platform is dual-tilting, meaning that it is able to tilt each propeller independently during flight, along two orthogonal axis. We present a hierarchical control structure composed of a high-level controller generating the required wrench for the tracking task, and a control allocation law ensuring that the actuators produce such wrench. The allocator imposes desired first-order dynamics on the actuators set, and exploits system redundancy to optimize the actuators state with respect to a given objective function. Unlike other studies on the subject, we explicitly model actuator saturation and provide theoretical insights on its effect on control performances. We also investigate the role of propeller tilt angles, by imposing asymmetric shapes in the objective function. Numerical simulations are presented to validate the allocation strategy.

</details>


### [117] [GraspSense: Physically Grounded Grasp and Grip Planning for a Dexterous Robotic Hand via Language-Guided Perception and Force Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05697)
*Elizaveta Semenyakina,Ivan Snegirev,Mariya Lezina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Safina Gulyamova,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于局部允许接触载荷图的机器人抓取选择与力调节管道，将灵巧操作从纯几何问题转化为物理基础的联合规划问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取规划器通常将物体表面视为结构均匀的，但接触在薄弱区域可能导致物体损坏。需要物理基础的接触策略来考虑物体空间非均匀的机械特性

Method: 基于局部允许接触载荷图的五指机器人手抓取选择与力调节管道：使用SAM3D重建3D几何，在Isaac Sim中导入模型，通过物理信息几何分析计算力图（编码每个表面位置在不发生变形情况下的最大横向接触力），基于几何有效性和任务目标一致性筛选抓取候选，使用力图感知准则对候选进行重新排序，阻抗控制器根据接触点局部允许力缩放每个手指的刚度

Result: 在纸杯、塑料杯和玻璃杯上的验证表明，该方法能一致选择结构更强的接触区域，并将抓握力保持在安全范围内

Conclusion: 该工作将灵巧操作从纯几何问题重新定义为物理基础的抓取选择与抓握执行的联合规划问题，为未来人形系统提供了解决方案

Abstract: Dexterous robotic manipulation requires more than geometrically valid grasps: it demands physically grounded contact strategies that account for the spatially non-uniform mechanical properties of the object. However, existing grasp planners typically treat the surface as structurally homogeneous, even though contact in a weak region can damage the object despite a geometrically perfect grasp. We present a pipeline for grasp selection and force regulation in a five-fingered robotic hand, based on a map of locally admissible contact loads. From an operator command, the system identifies the target object, reconstructs its 3D geometry using SAM3D, and imports the model into Isaac Sim. A physics-informed geometric analysis then computes a force map that encodes the maximum lateral contact force admissible at each surface location without deformation. Grasp candidates are filtered by geometric validity and task-goal consistency. When multiple candidates are comparable under classical metrics, they are re-ranked using a force-map-aware criterion that favors grasps with contacts in mechanically admissible regions. An impedance controller scales the stiffness of each finger according to the locally admissible force at the contact point, enabling safe and reliable grasp execution. Validation on paper, plastic, and glass cups shows that the proposed approach consistently selects structurally stronger contact regions and keeps grip forces within safe bounds. In this way, the work reframes dexterous manipulation from a purely geometric problem into a physically grounded joint planning problem of grasp selection and grip execution for future humanoid systems.

</details>


### [118] [Hazard Management in Robot-Assisted Mammography Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05749)
*Ioannis Stefanakos,Roisin Bradley,Radu Calinescu,Beverley Townsend,Tianyuan Wang,Jihong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于乳腺X光检查辅助机器人MammoBot的危险管理方法，结合利益相关者引导的过程建模、SHARD和STPA分析，识别临床环境中人机交互的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人和具身AI系统在临床环境中具有改善护理可及性和质量的潜力，但在与脆弱患者密切接触时引入了重大安全风险。需要系统化的安全设计方法来确保辅助机器人系统的安全性。

Method: 采用利益相关者引导的过程建模与SHARD（软件危险分析与设计解决）和STPA（系统理论过程分析）相结合的方法。首先与临床医生、机器人专家和患者代表协作定义机器人辅助工作流程，然后应用SHARD识别技术和程序偏差，使用STPA分析用户交互产生的非安全控制行为。

Result: 分析结果显示，许多危险并非来自组件故障，而是源于时间不匹配、过早行动和系统状态误解。这些危险被转化为精细化和额外的安全要求，约束系统行为并减少对正确人类计时或解释的依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种结构化且可追溯的安全驱动设计方法，对临床环境中的辅助机器人系统具有潜在适用性，强调了从早期开发阶段整合安全分析的重要性。

Abstract: Robotic and embodied-AI systems have the potential to improve accessibility and quality of care in clinical settings, but their deployment in close physical contact with vulnerable patients introduces significant safety risks. This paper presents a hazard management methodology for MammoBot, an assistive robotic system designed to support patients during X-ray mammography. To ensure safety from early development stages, we combine stakeholder-guided process modelling with Software Hazard Analysis and Resolution in Design (SHARD) and System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). The robot-assisted workflow is defined collaboratively with clinicians, roboticists, and patient representatives to capture key human-robot interactions. SHARD is applied to identify technical and procedural deviations, while STPA is used to analyse unsafe control actions arising from user interaction. The results show that many hazards arise not from component failures, but from timing mismatches, premature actions, and misinterpretation of system state. These hazards are translated into refined and additional safety requirements that constrain system behaviour and reduce reliance on correct human timing or interpretation alone. The work demonstrates a structured and traceable approach to safety-driven design with potential applicability to assistive robotic systems in clinical environments.

</details>


### [119] [Precise Aggressive Aerial Maneuvers with Sensorimotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05828)
*Tianyue Wu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Junxiao Lin,Tianyang Chen,Yuze Wu,Zhichao Han,Zhiyang Liu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的端到端传感器融合策略，使四旋翼无人机能够在仅有轻量级机载传感器的情况下，精确执行通过狭窄缝隙的激进机动，包括未知位置和方向的静态缝隙以及动态移动缝隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四旋翼无人机在利用其机动性方面存在关键瓶颈：需要精确执行激进机动（如通过狭窄缝隙）来扩展可访问区域，但现有方法难以在仅有轻量级机载传感器的情况下实现SE(3)约束下的精确控制。

Method: 开发传感器融合策略，将机载视觉和本体感知直接映射到低级控制指令；使用强化学习在仿真中进行端到端策略蒸馏；采用基于模型规划器生成的轨迹进行初始化，解决模型无关RL在受限解空间中的探索难题；精心设计仿真到现实的迁移方案。

Result: 方法使四旋翼能够以5厘米的间隙通过倾斜达90度的矩形缝隙，无需知道缝隙位置或方向；能够反应式地通过动态移动缝隙；在紧密排列的狭窄缝隙轨道上验证有效；策略可适应几何多样的缝隙，无需手动定义穿越姿态和视觉特征。

Conclusion: 提出的基于强化学习的传感器融合策略成功解决了四旋翼无人机在仅有轻量级机载传感器情况下执行激进缝隙穿越机动的问题，展示了高重复性和适应性，为无人机在复杂环境中的自主导航提供了灵活解决方案。

Abstract: Precise aggressive maneuvers with lightweight onboard sensors remains a key bottleneck in fully exploiting the maneuverability of drones. Such maneuvers are critical for expanding the systems' accessible area by navigating through narrow openings in the environment. Among the most relevant problems, a representative one is aggressive traversal through narrow gaps with quadrotors under SE(3) constraints, which require the quadrotors to leverage a momentary tilted attitude and the asymmetry of the airframe to navigate through gaps. In this paper, we achieve such maneuvers by developing sensorimotor policies directly mapping onboard vision and proprioception into low-level control commands. The policies are trained using reinforcement learning (RL) with end-to-end policy distillation in simulation. We mitigate the fundamental hardness of model-free RL's exploration on the restricted solution space with an initialization strategy leveraging trajectories generated by a model-based planner. Careful sim-to-real design allows the policy to control a quadrotor through narrow gaps with low clearances and high repeatability. For instance, the proposed method enables a quadrotor to navigate a rectangular gap at a 5 cm clearance, tilted at up to 90-degree orientation, without knowledge of the gap's position or orientation. Without training on dynamic gaps, the policy can reactively servo the quadrotor to traverse through a moving gap. The proposed method is also validated by training and deploying policies on challenging tracks of narrow gaps placed closely. The flexibility of the policy learning method is demonstrated by developing policies for geometrically diverse gaps, without relying on manually defined traversal poses and visual features.

</details>


### [120] [BiCoord: A Bimanual Manipulation Benchmark towards Long-Horizon Spatial-Temporal Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05831)
*Xingyu Peng,Chen Gao,Liankai Jin,Annan Li,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCoord是一个用于长视野、紧密协调的双臂操作基准测试，包含需要连续臂间依赖和动态角色交换的多样化任务，并提出了从时间、空间和时空角度评估协调性的量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂操作基准测试（如RoboTwin和RLBench2）主要关注短视野和松散协调的任务，无法捕捉真实世界双臂行为中固有的时空耦合特性，因此需要开发能够评估长视野、紧密协调操作的基准测试。

Method: 提出了BiCoord基准测试，包含多样化的长视野任务，这些任务需要连续的双臂依赖和动态角色交换。同时开发了一套量化指标，从时间、空间和时空三个维度系统评估双臂协调性。

Result: 实验结果表明，代表性的操作策略（如DP、RDT、Pi0和OpenVLA-OFT）在长持续时间、高度耦合的任务上表现不佳，揭示了实现长视野、紧密协调任务的基本挑战。

Conclusion: BiCoord为研究长视野协作操作提供了基础，能够激发未来关于协调感知机器人学习的研究，所有数据集、代码和补充材料均已公开。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation, i.e., the coordinated use of two robotic arms to complete tasks, is essential for achieving human-level dexterity in robotics. Recent simulation benchmarks, e.g., RoboTwin and RLBench2, have advanced data-driven learning for bimanual manipulation. However, existing tasks are short-horizon and only loosely coordinated, failing to capture the spatial-temporal coupling inherent in real-world bimanual behaviors. To address this gap, we introduce BiCoord, a benchmark for long-horizon and tightly coordinated bimanual manipulation. Specifically, BiCoord comprises diverse tasks that require continuous inter-arm dependency and dynamic role exchange across multiple sub-goals. Also, we propose a suite of quantitative metrics that evaluate coordination from temporal, spatial, and spatial-temporal perspectives, enabling systematic measurement of bimanual cooperation. Experimental results show that representative manipulation policies, e.g., DP, RDT, Pi0, and OpenVLA-OFT, struggle with long-duration and highly coupled tasks, revealing fundamental challenges in achieving long-horizon and tight coordination tasks. We hope BiCoord can serve as a foundation for studying long-horizon cooperative manipulation and inspire future research on coordination-aware robotic learning. All datasets, codes and supplements could be found at https://buaa-colalab.github.io/BiCoord/.

</details>


### [121] [Dialogue based Interactive Explanations for Safety Decisions in Human Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05896)
*Yifan Xu,Xiao Zhan,Akilu Yunusa Kaltungo,Ming Shan Ng,Tsukasa Ishizawa,Kota Fujimoto,Clara Cheung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于对话的框架，用于解释人机协作中的安全决策，将解释与基于约束的安全评估紧密结合，支持因果、对比和反事实查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人在安全关键环境中运行，仅安全行动已不足够，还需让人类协作伙伴理解其安全决策。当前人机协作中，由内部安全约束触发的行为（如停止或切换模式）对附近工作人员不透明。

Method: 提出基于对话的交互式安全决策解释框架，将解释与基于约束的安全评估紧密结合，使用与行为选择相同的状态和约束表示。解释直接来自记录决策轨迹，支持因果、对比和反事实查询。反事实推理在固定认证安全参数下有界评估，确保交互探索不放松操作保证。

Result: 在建筑机器人场景中实例化该框架，提供结构化操作轨迹，展示约束感知对话如何澄清安全干预并支持协调任务恢复。

Conclusion: 通过将解释视为安全控制的操作接口，这项工作为人机协作中交互式、安全感知的自主性提供了设计视角。

Abstract: As robots increasingly operate in shared, safety critical environments, acting safely is no longer sufficient robots must also make their safety decisions intelligible to human collaborators. In human robot collaboration (HRC), behaviours such as stopping or switching modes are often triggered by internal safety constraints that remain opaque to nearby workers. We present a dialogue based framework for interactive explanation of safety decisions in HRC. The approach tightly couples explanation with constraint based safety evaluation, grounding dialogue in the same state and constraint representations that govern behaviour selection. Explanations are derived directly from the recorded decision trace, enabling users to pose causal ("Why?"), contrastive ("Why not?"), and counterfactual ("What if?") queries about safety interventions. Counterfactual reasoning is evaluated in a bounded manner under fixed, certified safety parameters, ensuring that interactive exploration does not relax operational guarantees. We instantiate the framework in a construction robotics scenario and provide a structured operational trace illustrating how constraint aware dialogue clarifies safety interventions and supports coordinated task recovery. By treating explanation as an operational interface to safety control, this work advances a design perspective for interactive, safety aware autonomy in HRC.

</details>


### [122] [You're Pushing My Buttons: Instrumented Learning of Gentle Button Presses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05954)
*Raman Talwar,Remko Proesmans,Thomas Lips,Andreas Verleysen,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用指尖麦克风采集接触音频，通过按钮状态信号作为特权监督微调音频编码器为接触事件检测器，结合模仿学习策略，在推理时仅使用视觉和音频，实现接触丰富操作策略学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从摄像头和本体感知学习接触丰富的操作很困难，因为接触事件只能被部分观测。需要测试训练时仪器化（物体传感器化）是否能提高策略性能，同时不产生部署时的依赖

Method: 以按钮按压为测试平台，使用指尖麦克风捕捉接触相关音频。使用仪器化的按钮状态信号作为特权监督，将音频编码器微调为接触事件检测器。将得到的表征与模仿学习结合，采用三种策略，使策略在推理时仅使用视觉和音频

Result: 按钮按压成功率在不同方法间相似，但仪器化引导的音频表征一致地减少了接触力。这些结果支持仪器化作为学习接触丰富操作策略的实用训练时辅助目标

Conclusion: 训练时仪器化可以作为学习接触丰富操作策略的有效辅助目标，通过音频表征减少接触力，同时避免部署时对额外传感器的依赖

Abstract: Learning contact-rich manipulation is difficult from cameras and proprioception alone because contact events are only partially observed. We test whether training-time instrumentation, i.e., object sensorisation, can improve policy performance without creating deployment-time dependencies. Specifically, we study button pressing as a testbed and use a microphone fingertip to capture contact-relevant audio. We use an instrumented button-state signal as privileged supervision to fine-tune an audio encoder into a contact event detector. We combine the resulting representation with imitation learning using three strategies, such that the policy only uses vision and audio during inference. Button press success rates are similar across methods, but instrumentation-guided audio representations consistently reduce contact force. These results support instrumentation as a practical training-time auxiliary objective for learning contact-rich manipulation policies.

</details>


### [123] [Automating Manual Tasks through Intuitive Robot Programming and Cognitive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05978)
*Bijan Kavousian,Petar Tesic,Oliver Petrovic,Christian Brecher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于自然语言和手势的机器人编程框架，利用LLM和CV技术将人类自然交互转化为机器人程序，通过澄清问题和可视化反馈确保安全性和用户接受度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类自然交互启发，旨在为终端用户提供直观的机器人编程方式，解决传统编程方法复杂、不直观的问题，提升机器人编程的可访问性和用户体验

Method: 结合大型语言模型（LLMs）和计算机视觉（CV），将自然语言指令和手势转换为机器人程序，通过澄清问题和视觉反馈机制让用户审查和调整生成的程序

Result: 开发了一个能够理解自然交互并生成机器人程序的系统，通过交互式反馈机制确保程序的安全性和透明度，提高了用户对机器人编程的接受度

Conclusion: 该框架为终端用户提供了直观的机器人编程方法，通过自然交互和反馈机制实现了安全、透明的编程过程，为机器人编程的人机交互开辟了新方向

Abstract: This paper presents a novel concept for intuitive end-user programming of robots, inspired by natural interaction between humans. Natural language and supportive gestures are translated into robot programs using large language models (LLMs) and computer vision (CV). Through equally natural system feedback in the form of clarification questions and visual representations, the generated program can be reviewed and adjusted, thereby ensuring safety, transparency, and user acceptance.

</details>


### [124] [Force Polytope-Based Cant-Angle Selection for Tilting Hexarotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05998)
*Alberto Piccina,Massimiliano Bertoni,Angelo Cenedese,Giulia Michieletto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种轻量级控制框架，用于星形相互依赖倾斜六旋翼无人机在执行交互任务时的倾角优化选择，通过离线计算的零力矩力多面体查找表平衡效率和平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从机动性角度看，倾斜多旋翼无人机的主要优势在于可执行力矩的动态可变性，这是物理交互任务的关键资产。因此，倾角选择需要优化以确保高性能，同时避免突变并保持实际可行性。

Method: 提出轻量级控制框架，使用离线计算的零力矩力多面体查找表来识别期望控制力下的可行倾角，并通过平衡效率和平滑性选择最优倾角。该框架与几何全姿态控制器集成。

Result: 结果显示计算时间显著减少，姿态跟踪性能改善，执行效率具有竞争力。在Simscape中进行的完整墙壁检查任务的物理模拟进一步证实了该策略在交互场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的控制框架通过离线查找表优化倾角选择，实现了计算效率提升和性能改善，验证了在物理交互任务中的可行性，为倾斜多旋翼无人机的交互应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: From a maneuverability perspective, the main advantage of tilting multirotor UAVs lies in the dynamic variability of the feasible executable wrench, which represents a key asset for physical interaction tasks. Accordingly, cant-angle selection should be optimized to ensure high performance while avoiding abrupt variations and preserving real-world feasibility. In this context, this work proposes a lightweight control framework for star-shaped interdependent cant-tilting hexarotor UAVs performing interaction tasks. The method uses an offline-computed look-up table of zero-moment force polytopes to identify feasible cant angles for a desired control force and select the optimal one by balancing efficiency and smoothness. The framework is integrated with a geometric full-pose controller and validated through Monte Carlo simulations in MATLAB/Simulink and compared against a baseline strategy. The results show a significant reduction in computation time, together with improved pose-tracking performance and competitive actuation efficiency. A final physics-based simulation of a complete wall inspection task in Simscape further confirms the feasibility of the proposed strategy in interacting scenarios.

</details>


### [125] [A Co-Design Framework for High-Performance Jumping of a Five-Bar Monoped with Actuator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06025)
*Aastha Mishra,Aman Singh,Shishir Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于平面闭链五杆单足机器人的协同设计框架，联合优化机械设计、电机/变速箱参数和控制参数，以实现动态跳跃性能最大化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计研究大多关注连杆尺寸和传动比优化，忽略了详细的执行器设计（特别是电机和变速箱参数优化），且主要局限于开链机构。需要开发能同时优化机械设计、执行器参数和控制参数的框架，以提升腿式机器人的跳跃性能和能效

Method: 采用两阶段优化方法：1) 执行器优化阶段生成从齿轮比到执行器质量、效率和峰值扭矩的映射；2) 协同设计优化阶段使用CMA-ES算法联合优化机器人设计和控制参数。框架针对平面闭链五杆单足机器人，目标是最大化跳跃距离同时最小化机械能耗

Result: 仿真结果显示，与基准设计相比，跳跃距离提升约42%，机械能耗降低15.8%。该框架能有效识别最优的设计、执行器和控制参数，实现高性能和能量高效的平面跳跃

Conclusion: 提出的协同设计框架成功解决了现有研究的局限性，通过联合优化机械设计、执行器参数和控制参数，显著提升了平面闭链单足机器人的跳跃性能和能量效率，为高性能腿式机器人的设计提供了有效方法

Abstract: The performance of legged robots depends strongly on both mechanical design and control, motivating co-design approaches that jointly optimize these parameters. However, most existing co-design studies focus on optimizing link dimensions and transmission ratios while neglecting detailed actuator design, particularly motor and gearbox parameter optimization, and are largely limited to serial open-chain mechanisms. In this work, we present a co-design framework for a planar closed-chain five-bar monoped that jointly optimizes mechanical design, motor and gearbox parameters, and control parameters for dynamic jumping. The objective is to maximize jump distance while minimizing mechanical energy consumption. The framework uses a two-stage optimization approach, where actuator optimization generates a mapping from gear ratio to actuator mass, efficiency, and peak torque, which is then used in co-design optimization of the robot design and control using CMA-ES. Simulation results show an improvement of approximately 42% in jump distance and a 15.8% reduction in mechanical energy consumption compared to a nominal design, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in identifying optimal design, actuator, and control parameters for high-performance and energy-efficient planar jumping.

</details>


### [126] [HiPolicy: Hierarchical Multi-Frequency Action Chunking for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06067)
*Jiyao Zhang,Zimu Han,Junhan Wang,Xionghao Wu,Shihong Lin,Jinzhou Li,Hongwei Fan,Ruihai Wu,Dongjiang Li,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiPolicy：一种分层多频率动作分块框架，通过联合预测不同频率的动作序列来同时捕获粗粒度高层规划和精确反应运动，解决了模仿学习中长时依赖建模与细粒度闭环控制之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习面临一个基本权衡：建模长时依赖与实现细粒度闭环控制之间的冲突。现有的固定频率动作分块方法难以同时实现这两个目标，因此需要一种能够兼顾高层规划和精确控制的新框架。

Method: 提出HiPolicy分层多频率动作分块框架：1）联合预测不同频率的动作序列，分别捕获粗粒度高层计划和精确反应运动；2）从历史观测中提取并融合与每个频率对齐的分层特征，用于多频率分块生成；3）引入基于熵的引导执行机制，根据动作不确定性自适应平衡长时规划与细粒度控制。

Result: 在多样化的模拟基准测试和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，HiPolicy可以无缝集成到现有的2D和3D生成策略中，在显著提高执行效率的同时，持续提升性能表现。

Conclusion: HiPolicy通过分层多频率动作分块框架有效解决了模仿学习中长时依赖建模与细粒度控制之间的权衡问题，为机器人模仿学习提供了一种既能进行高层规划又能实现精确反应控制的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic imitation learning faces a fundamental trade-off between modeling long-horizon dependencies and enabling fine-grained closed-loop control. Existing fixed-frequency action chunking approaches struggle to achieve both. Building on this insight, we propose HiPolicy, a hierarchical multi-frequency action chunking framework that jointly predicts action sequences at different frequencies to capture both coarse high-level plans and precise reactive motions. We extract and fuse hierarchical features from history observations aligned to each frequency for multi-frequency chunk generation, and introduce an entropy-guided execution mechanism that adaptively balances long-horizon planning with fine-grained control based on action uncertainty. Experiments on diverse simulated benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks show that HiPolicy can be seamlessly integrated into existing 2D and 3D generative policies, delivering consistent improvements in performance while significantly enhancing execution efficiency.

</details>


### [127] [Intuitive Human-Robot Interaction: Development and Evaluation of a Gesture-Based User Interface for Object Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06073)
*Bijan Kavousian,Oliver Petrovic,Werner Herfs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于手势的用户界面，使用指向和点击手势进行物体选择，通过实验评估准确性和选择时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手势是人类之间自然的交流形式，可以用于人机交互，特别是人机协作场景

Method: 开发基于手势的用户界面，使用指向和点击手势进行物体选择，通过20名参与者的实验进行评估

Result: 实验评估了准确性和选择时间，展示了高效协作的潜力

Conclusion: 基于手势的界面在人机交互中具有潜力，能够实现高效的物体选择和协作

Abstract: Gestures are a natural form of communication between humans and can also be leveraged for human-robot interaction. This work presents a gesture-based user interface for object selection using pointing and click gestures. An experiment with 20 participants evaluates accuracy and selection time, demonstrating the potential for efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [128] [Learning-Guided Force-Feedback Model Predictive Control with Obstacle Avoidance for Robotic Deburring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06133)
*Krzysztof Wojciechowski,Ege Gursoy,Arthur Haffemayer,Sebastien Kleff,Vincent Bonnet,Florent Lamiraux,Nicolas Mansard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合力反馈MPC与扩散运动先验的框架，用于解决碰撞约束下接触密集型工业任务（如去毛刺）的挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC方法在扭矩控制机器人应用中常忽略实时力反馈，难以处理碰撞约束下的接触密集型工业任务。去毛刺任务需要精确工具插入、稳定力调节和无碰撞圆周运动，这超出了标准MPC的能力范围

Method: 提出集成力反馈MPC与扩散运动先验的框架。扩散模型作为运动策略的记忆，提供跨多个任务实例的鲁棒初始化和适应；MPC确保安全执行，具有显式力跟踪、扭矩可行性和碰撞避免

Result: 在扭矩控制机械臂上进行工业去毛刺任务验证。实验显示可靠的工具插入、精确的法向力跟踪，以及在难以到达的配置和障碍约束下仍能实现圆周去毛刺运动

Conclusion: 这是首次将扩散运动先验与力反馈MPC集成，用于碰撞感知的接触密集型工业任务。该框架成功解决了传统MPC在工业去毛刺等复杂任务中的局限性

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) is widely used for torque-controlled robots, but classical formulations often neglect real-time force feedback and struggle with contact-rich industrial tasks under collision constraints. Deburring in particular requires precise tool insertion, stable force regulation, and collision-free circular motions in challenging configurations, which exceeds the capability of standard MPC pipelines. We propose a framework that integrates force-feedback MPC with diffusion-based motion priors to address these challenges. The diffusion model serves as a memory of motion strategies, providing robust initialization and adaptation across multiple task instances, while MPC ensures safe execution with explicit force tracking, torque feasibility, and collision avoidance. We validate our approach on a torque-controlled manipulator performing industrial deburring tasks. Experiments demonstrate reliable tool insertion, accurate normal force tracking, and circular deburring motions even in hard-to-reach configurations and under obstacle constraints. To our knowledge, this is the first integration of diffusion motion priors with force-feedback MPC for collision-aware, contact-rich industrial tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Pramana: Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for Epistemic Reasoning through Navya-Nyaya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04937)
*Sharath Sathish*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Pramana方法，通过基于2500年印度Navya-Nyaya逻辑框架微调LLMs，教授显式认识论方法，改善大语言模型的系统性推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成流畅文本时存在系统性推理困难，容易产生无根据的幻觉。当苹果研究人员在数学问题中添加无关上下文时，LLM性能下降65%，暴露了其表面推理下的脆弱模式匹配。这种认识论差距（无法将主张基于可追溯证据）限制了AI在需要论证的领域中的可靠性。

Method: 引入Pramana方法，通过基于Navya-Nyaya逻辑框架微调LLMs。Navya-Nyaya强制执行结构化6阶段推理：怀疑分析、证据源识别、5成员三段论（含普遍规则）、反事实验证、谬误检测、以及区分知识与假设的确定。在55个Nyaya结构化逻辑问题（约束满足、布尔SAT、多步演绎）上微调Llama 3.2-3B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B模型。

Result: 第一阶段在保留评估集上达到100%语义正确性，尽管严格格式遵循率仅为40%，表明模型即使在不完美的结构强制下也能内化推理内容。消融研究显示格式提示和温度对性能有重要影响，不同阶段的最优配置不同。所有模型、数据集和训练基础设施已在Hugging Face上发布。

Conclusion: 将逻辑与认识论结合的方法为标准推理方法提供了缺失的认知支架，为解决LLMs的认识论差距提供了新途径。该方法展示了即使在不完美的结构遵循下，模型也能学习推理内容，为AI推理的认知框架研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models produce fluent text but struggle with systematic reasoning, often hallucinating confident but unfounded claims. When Apple researchers added irrelevant context to mathematical problems, LLM performance degraded by 65% Apple Machine Learning Research, exposing brittle pattern-matching beneath apparent reasoning. This epistemic gap, the inability to ground claims in traceable evidence, limits AI reliability in domains requiring justification. We introduce Pramana, a novel approach that teaches LLMs explicit epistemological methodology by fine-tuning on Navya-Nyaya logic, a 2,500-year-old Indian reasoning framework. Unlike generic chain-of-thought prompting, Navya-Nyaya enforces structured 6-phase reasoning: SAMSHAYA (doubt analysis), PRAMANA (evidence source identification), PANCHA AVAYAVA (5-member syllogism with universal rules), TARKA (counterfactual verification), HETVABHASA (fallacy detection), and NIRNAYA (ascertainment distinguishing knowledge from hypothesis). This integration of logic and epistemology provides cognitive scaffolding absent from standard reasoning approaches. We fine-tune Llama 3.2-3B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B on 55 Nyaya-structured logical problems (constraint satisfaction, Boolean SAT, multi-step deduction). Stage 1 achieves 100% semantic correctness on held-out evaluation despite only 40% strict format adherence revealing that models internalize reasoning content even when structural enforcement is imperfect. Ablation studies show format prompting and temperature critically affect performance, with optimal configurations differing by stage. We release all models, datasets, and training infrastructure on Hugging Face to enable further research on epistemic frameworks for AI reasoning.

</details>


### [130] [Operational Noncommutativity in Sequential Metacognitive Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04938)
*Enso O. Torres Alegre,Diana E. Mora Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个操作框架来区分认知顺序效应中的经典状态变化与结构性非交换性，证明了顺序依赖性排除了忠实的布尔交换表示，并建立了违反经典非侵入性解释的测试约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元认知过程本质上是顺序性的，但尚不清楚认知中的顺序效应是反映经典状态变化还是揭示更深层次的结构性非交换性。需要建立一个明确区分这两种可能性的操作框架。

Method: 将元认知评估建模为作用于内部状态空间的状态转换操作，将评估反作用与可观测输出分离。引入反事实确定性和评估非侵入性两个假设，推导出顺序相关性的可测试约束。提供了一个明确的三维旋转模型和数值示例，并概述了涉及感知决策后顺序信心、错误可能性和知晓感判断的行为范式。

Result: 证明了顺序依赖性排除了任何忠实的布尔交换表示。建立了违反经典非侵入性解释的测试约束，这些约束的违反排除了任何经典非侵入性解释，并证明了真正的非交换性。提供了展示这种违反的三维旋转模型和数值示例。

Conclusion: 该框架为区分认知顺序效应中的经典状态变化与结构性非交换性提供了操作工具，证明了真正的非交换性可以通过违反特定的顺序相关性约束来认证。该框架是纯粹操作性和代数性的，不涉及量子物理基质。

Abstract: Metacognition, understood as the monitoring and regulation of one's own cognitive processes, is inherently sequential: an agent evaluates an internal state, updates it, and may then re-evaluate under modified criteria. Order effects in cognition are well documented, yet it remains unclear whether such effects reflect classical state changes or reveal a deeper structural non-commutativity. We develop an operational framework that makes this distinction explicit. In our formulation, metacognitive evaluations are modeled as state-transforming operations acting on an internal state space with probabilistic readouts, thereby separating evaluation back-action from observable output.
  We show that order dependence prevents any faithful Boolean-commutative representation. We then address a stronger question: can observed order effects always be explained by enlarging the state space with classical latent variables? To formalize this issue, we introduce two assumptions, counterfactual definiteness and evaluation non-invasiveness, under which the existence of a joint distribution over all sequential readouts implies a family of testable constraints on pairwise sequential correlations. Violation of these constraints rules out any classical non-invasive account and certifies what we call genuine non-commutativity.
  We provide an explicit three-dimensional rotation model with fully worked numerical examples that exhibits such violations. We also outline a behavioral paradigm involving sequential confidence, error-likelihood, and feeling-of-knowing judgments following a perceptual decision, together with the corresponding empirical test. No claim is made regarding quantum physical substrates; the framework is purely operational and algebraic.

</details>


### [131] [Proximity Measure of Information Object Features for Solving the Problem of Their Identification in Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04939)
*Volodymyr Yuzefovych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种新的定量-定性特征邻近度度量方法，用于判断来自不同信息源的数据是否属于同一物理对象，无需特征值转换即可处理混合类型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多源信息融合中判断数据是否属于同一物理对象的问题，现有方法通常需要特征值转换才能处理混合类型特征，这可能导致信息损失或失真。

Method: 采用概率度量分析定量特征邻近度，可能性度量分析定性特征邻近度，组合成统一的定量-定性邻近度度量，并验证其满足度量公理要求。

Result: 提出的度量方法无需特征值转换即可处理混合类型特征，满足度量公理要求，并开发了多种基于多样化特征组的信息对象邻近度度量变体。

Conclusion: 该定量-定性邻近度度量方法为多源信息融合提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够处理存在确定误差的混合类型特征，具有理论合理性和实际可行性。

Abstract: The paper considers a new quantitative-qualitative proximity measure for the features of information objects, where data enters a common information resource from several sources independently. The goal is to determine the possibility of their relation to the same physical object (observation object). The proposed measure accounts for the possibility of differences in individual feature values - both quantitative and qualitative - caused by existing determination errors. To analyze the proximity of quantitative feature values, the author employs a probabilistic measure; for qualitative features, a measure of possibility is used. The paper demonstrates the feasibility of the proposed measure by checking its compliance with the axioms required of any measure. Unlike many known measures, the proposed approach does not require feature value transformation to ensure comparability. The work also proposes several variants of measures to determine the proximity of information objects (IO) based on a group of diverse features.

</details>


### [132] [ReVEL: Multi-Turn Reflective LLM-Guided Heuristic Evolution via Structured Performance Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04940)
*Cuong Van Duc,Minh Nguyen Dinh Tuan,Tam Vu Duc,Tung Vu Duy,Son Nguyen Van,Hanh Nguyen Thi,Binh Huynh Thi Thanh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReVEL框架通过多轮反思性LLM引导的启发式进化，结合性能分组和反馈驱动反思，自动设计NP难组合优化问题的鲁棒启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的启发式设计主要依赖一次性代码生成，产生脆弱的启发式算法，未能充分利用模型的多轮推理能力。需要开发能够嵌入LLM作为交互式多轮推理器的混合框架。

Method: 提出ReVEL框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 性能-特征分组：将候选启发式聚类为行为一致的组，为LLM提供紧凑且信息丰富的反馈；(2) 多轮反馈驱动反思：LLM分析组级行为并生成有针对性的启发式改进。这些改进由基于进化算法的元控制器选择性地集成和验证，自适应地平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在标准组合优化基准测试中，ReVEL一致地产生更鲁棒和多样化的启发式算法，相比强基线实现了统计显著的改进。

Conclusion: 多轮推理结合结构化分组为自动启发式设计提供了一个原则性范式，展示了LLM作为交互式推理器在进化算法框架中的有效性。

Abstract: Designing effective heuristics for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems remains a challenging and expertise-intensive task. Existing applications of large language models (LLMs) primarily rely on one-shot code synthesis, yielding brittle heuristics that underutilize the models' capacity for iterative reasoning. We propose ReVEL: Multi-Turn Reflective LLM-Guided Heuristic Evolution via Structured Performance Feedback, a hybrid framework that embeds LLMs as interactive, multi-turn reasoners within an evolutionary algorithm (EA). The core of ReVEL lies in two mechanisms: (i) performance-profile grouping, which clusters candidate heuristics into behaviorally coherent groups to provide compact and informative feedback to the LLM; and (ii) multi-turn, feedback-driven reflection, through which the LLM analyzes group-level behaviors and generates targeted heuristic refinements. These refinements are selectively integrated and validated by an EA-based meta-controller that adaptively balances exploration and exploitation. Experiments on standard combinatorial optimization benchmarks show that ReVEL consistently produces heuristics that are more robust and diverse, achieving statistically significant improvements over strong baselines. Our results highlight multi-turn reasoning with structured grouping as a principled paradigm for automated heuristic design.

</details>


### [133] [Algebraic Structure Discovery for Real World Combinatorial Optimisation Problems: A General Framework from Abstract Algebra to Quotient Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04941)
*Min Sun,Federica Storti,Valentina Martino,Miguel Gonzalez-Andrades,Tony Kam-Thong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个通用框架，通过识别代数结构、构建商空间来缩小搜索空间，提高组合优化问题的求解效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组合优化问题隐藏着代数结构，一旦暴露这些结构，可以缩小搜索空间并提高找到全局最优解的机会

Method: 提出四步框架：(1)识别代数结构，(2)形式化操作，(3)构建商空间以折叠冗余表示，(4)直接在简化空间上优化。针对规则组合任务，证明合取规则形成幺半群，通过特征向量编码建立与布尔超立方体的同构

Result: 在真实临床数据和合成基准测试中，商空间感知的遗传算法在48%到77%的运行中恢复全局最优解，而标准方法仅为35%到37%，同时在等价类间保持多样性

Conclusion: 暴露和利用代数结构为更高效的组合优化提供了一条简单通用的途径

Abstract: Many combinatorial optimisation problems hide algebraic structures that, once exposed, shrink the search space and improve the chance of finding the global optimal solution. We present a general framework that (i) identifies algebraic structure, (ii) formalises operations, (iii) constructs quotient spaces that collapse redundant representations, and (iv) optimises directly over these reduced spaces. Across a broad family of rule-combination tasks (e.g., patient subgroup discovery and rule-based molecular screening), conjunctive rules form a monoid. Via a characteristic-vector encoding, we prove an isomorphism to the Boolean hypercube $\{0,1\}^n$ with bitwise OR, so logical AND in rules becomes bitwise OR in the encoding. This yields a principled quotient-space formulation that groups functionally equivalent rules and guides structure-aware search. On real clinical data and synthetic benchmarks, quotient-space-aware genetic algorithms recover the global optimum in 48% to 77% of runs versus 35% to 37% for standard approaches, while maintaining diversity across equivalence classes. These results show that exposing and exploiting algebraic structure offers a simple, general route to more efficient combinatorial optimisation.

</details>


### [134] [PaperOrchestra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated AI Research Paper Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05018)
*Yiwen Song,Yale Song,Tomas Pfister,Jinsung Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaperOrchestra：一个用于自动化AI研究论文写作的多智能体框架，能够将非结构化的预写作材料转化为可提交的LaTeX稿件，包含全面的文献综述和可视化内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化写作工具与特定实验流程紧密耦合，且生成的文献综述较为肤浅。AI驱动的科学发现中，将非结构化研究材料合成为手稿是一个重要但尚未充分探索的挑战。

Method: 提出了PaperOrchestra多智能体框架，能够灵活地将无约束的预写作材料转化为可提交的LaTeX稿件，包括全面的文献合成和生成可视化内容（如图表和概念图）。

Result: 在人类评估中，PaperOrchestra显著优于现有自动化基线，在文献综述质量方面获得50%-68%的绝对胜率优势，在整体手稿质量方面获得14%-38%的优势。同时提出了PaperWritingBench基准测试，包含200篇顶级AI会议论文的反向工程原始材料。

Conclusion: PaperOrchestra为自动化AI研究论文写作提供了一个有效的多智能体框架，能够生成高质量的文献综述和完整手稿，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Synthesizing unstructured research materials into manuscripts is an essential yet under-explored challenge in AI-driven scientific discovery. Existing autonomous writers are rigidly coupled to specific experimental pipelines, and produce superficial literature reviews. We introduce PaperOrchestra, a multi-agent framework for automated AI research paper writing. It flexibly transforms unconstrained pre-writing materials into submission-ready LaTeX manuscripts, including comprehensive literature synthesis and generated visuals, such as plots and conceptual diagrams. To evaluate performance, we present PaperWritingBench, the first standardized benchmark of reverse-engineered raw materials from 200 top-tier AI conference papers, alongside a comprehensive suite of automated evaluators. In side-by-side human evaluations, PaperOrchestra significantly outperforms autonomous baselines, achieving an absolute win rate margin of 50%-68% in literature review quality, and 14%-38% in overall manuscript quality.

</details>


### [135] [Part-Level 3D Gaussian Vehicle Generation with Joint and Hinge Axis Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05070)
*Shiyao Qian,Yuan Ren,Dongfeng Bai,Bingbing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出生成式框架从单张或多视角图像合成可动画的3D高斯车辆模型，解决现有静态生成方法在部件动画时产生变形的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真需要可动画的车辆模型，但现有CAD流程受限于库覆盖范围和固定模板，无法真实重建野外实例；感知算法越来越依赖车轮转向、车门开启等动态信息，需要可动画的车辆表示

Method: 引入部件边缘细化模块确保高斯分布独占所有权，以及运动学推理头预测可动部件的关节位置和铰链轴；从单张图像或稀疏多视角输入生成可动画的3D高斯车辆

Result: 实现了部件感知的仿真，在部件边界处减少变形，能够预测运动学参数，弥合静态生成与可动画车辆模型之间的差距

Conclusion: 提出的生成式框架能够从有限输入合成高质量可动画车辆模型，为自动驾驶仿真提供更真实的部件级动态表示

Abstract: Simulation is essential for autonomous driving, yet current frameworks often model vehicles as rigid assets and fail to capture part-level articulation. With perception algorithms increasingly leveraging dynamics such as wheel steering or door opening, realistic simulation requires animatable vehicle representations. Existing CAD-based pipelines are limited by library coverage and fixed templates, preventing faithful reconstruction of in-the-wild instances.
  We propose a generative framework that, from a single image or sparse multi-view input, synthesizes an animatable 3D Gaussian vehicle. Our method addresses two challenges: (i) large 3D asset generators are optimized for static quality but not articulation, leading to distortions at part boundaries when animated; and (ii) segmentation alone cannot provide the kinematic parameters required for motion. To overcome this, we introduce a part-edge refinement module that enforces exclusive Gaussian ownership and a kinematic reasoning head that predicts joint positions and hinge axes of movable parts. Together, these components enable faithful part-aware simulation, bridging the gap between static generation and animatable vehicle models.

</details>


### [136] [MMORF: A Multi-agent Framework for Designing Multi-objective Retrosynthesis Planning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05075)
*Frazier N. Baker,Trieu Nguyen,Reza Averly,Botao Yu,Daniel Adu-Ampratwum,Huan Sun,Xia Ning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMORF是一个用于多目标逆合成规划的模块化多智能体系统框架，通过灵活组合专业智能体来平衡质量、安全性和成本目标，在软约束和硬约束任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标逆合成规划需要动态平衡质量、安全性和成本等多个目标，传统方法难以有效整合这些目标。基于语言模型的多智能体系统通过专业智能体交互为这一问题提供了有前景的解决方案。

Method: 提出MMORF框架，包含模块化的智能体组件，可灵活组合配置成不同系统。基于该框架构建了两个代表性多智能体系统：MASIL（用于软约束任务）和RFAS（用于硬约束任务）。

Result: 在包含218个多目标逆合成规划任务的新基准上，MASIL在软约束任务上实现了优异的安全性和成本指标，经常帕累托支配基线路线；RFAS在硬约束任务上达到48.6%的成功率，优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: MMORF作为探索多目标逆合成规划多智能体系统的基础框架是有效的，为系统设计和评估提供了原则性方法，展示了多智能体系统在该领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-objective retrosynthesis planning is a critical chemistry task requiring dynamic balancing of quality, safety, and cost objectives. Language model-based multi-agent systems (MAS) offer a promising approach for this task: leveraging interactions of specialized agents to incorporate multiple objectives into retrosynthesis planning. We present MMORF, a framework for constructing MAS for multi-objective retrosynthesis planning. MMORF features modular agentic components, which can be flexibly combined and configured into different systems, enabling principled evaluation and comparison of different system designs. Using MMORF, we construct two representative MAS: MASIL and RFAS. On a newly curated benchmark consisting of 218 multi-objective retrosynthesis planning tasks, MASIL achieves strong safety and cost metrics on soft-constraint tasks, frequently Pareto-dominating baseline routes, while RFAS achieves a 48.6% success rate on hard-constraint tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Together, these results show the effectiveness of MMORF as a foundational framework for exploring MAS for multi-objective retrosynthesis planning. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MMORF/.

</details>


### [137] [MedGemma 1.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05081)
*Andrew Sellergren,Chufan Gao,Fereshteh Mahvar,Timo Kohlberger,Fayaz Jamil,Madeleine Traverse,Alberto Tono,Bashir Sadjad,Lin Yang,Charles Lau,Liron Yatziv,Tiffany Chen,Bram Sterling,Kenneth Philbrick,Richa Tiwari,Yun Liu,Madhuram Jajoo,Chandrashekar Sankarapu,Swapnil Vispute,Harshad Purandare,Abhishek Bijay Mishra,Sam Schmidgall,Tao Tu,Anil Palepu,Chunjong Park,Tim Strother,Rahul Thapa,Yong Cheng,Preeti Singh,Kat Black,Yossi Matias,Katherine Chou,Avinatan Hassidim,Kavi Goel,Joelle Barral,Tris Warkentin,Shravya Shetty,Dale Webster,Sunny Virmani,David F. Steiner,Can Kirmizibayrak,Daniel Golden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedGemma 1.5 4B是MedGemma系列的最新模型，在MedGemma 1基础上扩展了高维医学影像处理、解剖定位、多时间点胸片分析和医疗文档理解能力，在多个医学AI任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个统一的医学AI架构，能够处理多种医疗模态数据，包括3D医学影像、病理切片、多时间点影像和医疗文档，为医疗AI系统提供更强大的基础模型。

Method: 在MedGemma 1基础上整合新功能：1）高维医学影像处理（CT/MRI体积和病理全切片图像）；2）通过边界框进行解剖定位；3）多时间点胸片分析；4）改进的医疗文档理解。采用新技术包括新的训练数据、长上下文3D体积切片和全切片病理采样。

Result: 相比MedGemma 1 4B，MedGemma 1.5 4B在多个任务上取得显著提升：3D MRI条件分类准确率提高11%，3D CT条件分类提高3%，全切片病理成像获得47%的宏观F1增益。解剖定位IoU提高35%，多时间点胸片分析宏观准确率4%。文本临床知识和推理方面，MedQA准确率提高5%，EHRQA准确率提高22%，在4个实验室报告信息提取数据集上平均获得18%宏观F1。

Conclusion: MedGemma 1.5作为一个强大的开源资源，为社区提供了改进的基础模型，开发者可以在此基础上构建下一代医疗AI系统。该模型在多种医疗模态任务上表现出色，是医疗AI领域的重要进展。

Abstract: We introduce MedGemma 1.5 4B, the latest model in the MedGemma collection. MedGemma 1.5 expands on MedGemma 1 by integrating additional capabilities: high-dimensional medical imaging (CT/MRI volumes and histopathology whole slide images), anatomical localization via bounding boxes, multi-timepoint chest X-ray analysis, and improved medical document understanding (lab reports, electronic health records). We detail the innovations required to enable these modalities within a single architecture, including new training data, long-context 3D volume slicing, and whole-slide pathology sampling. Compared to MedGemma 1 4B, MedGemma 1.5 4B demonstrates significant gains in these new areas, improving 3D MRI condition classification accuracy by 11% and 3D CT condition classification by 3% (absolute improvements). In whole slide pathology imaging, MedGemma 1.5 4B achieves a 47% macro F1 gain. Additionally, it improves anatomical localization with a 35% increase in Intersection over Union on chest X-rays and achieves a 4% macro accuracy for longitudinal (multi-timepoint) chest x-ray analysis. Beyond its improved multimodal performance over MedGemma 1, MedGemma 1.5 improves on text-based clinical knowledge and reasoning, improving by 5% on MedQA accuracy and 22% on EHRQA accuracy. It also achieves an average of 18% macro F1 on 4 different lab report information extraction datasets (EHR Datasets 2, 3, 4, and Mendeley Clinical Laboratory Test Reports). Taken together, MedGemma 1.5 serves as a robust, open resource for the community, designed as an improved foundation on which developers can create the next generation of medical AI systems. Resources and tutorials for building upon MedGemma 1.5 can be found at https://goo.gle/MedGemma.

</details>


### [138] [Non-monotonic causal discovery with Kolmogorov-Arnold Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05136)
*Jose L. Salmeron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KA-FCM：基于Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理的新型模糊认知图，用可学习的B样条函数替代标量权重，能够建模非单调因果关系，保持图结构可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FCM使用标量权重和单调激活函数，无法有效建模非单调因果依赖关系（如饱和效应、周期性动态），限制了其在复杂动态系统中的应用。

Method: 提出KA-FCM架构，基于Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理，将静态标量权重替换为位于模型边上的可学习单变量B样条函数，将非线性从节点聚合阶段直接转移到因果影响阶段。

Result: 在三个领域验证：非单调推理（Yerkes-Dodson定律）、符号回归和混沌时间序列预测。KA-FCM显著优于传统FCM架构，与MLP相比具有竞争力，同时保持图结构可解释性。

Conclusion: KA-FCM通过将标量权重替换为可学习函数，成功克服了传统FCM在建模非单调因果关系方面的限制，实现了高精度与可解释性的平衡，能够从学习到的边中显式提取数学规律。

Abstract: Fuzzy Cognitive Maps constitute a neuro-symbolic paradigm for modeling complex dynamic systems, widely adopted for their inherent interpretability and recurrent inference capabilities. However, the standard FCM formulation, characterized by scalar synaptic weights and monotonic activation functions, is fundamentally constrained in modeling non-monotonic causal dependencies, thereby limiting its efficacy in systems governed by saturation effects or periodic dynamics. To overcome this topological restriction, this research proposes the Kolmogorov-Arnold Fuzzy Cognitive Map (KA-FCM), a novel architecture that redefines the causal transmission mechanism. Drawing upon the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem, static scalar weights are replaced with learnable, univariate B-spline functions located on the model edges. This fundamental modification shifts the non-linearity from the nodes' aggregation phase directly to the causal influence phase. This modification allows for the modeling of arbitrary, non-monotonic causal relationships without increasing the graph density or introducing hidden layers. The proposed architecture is validated against both baselines (standard FCM trained with Particle Swarm Optimization) and universal black-box approximators (Multi-Layer Perceptron) across three distinct domains: non-monotonic inference (Yerkes-Dodson law), symbolic regression, and chaotic time-series forecasting. Experimental results demonstrate that KA-FCMs significantly outperform conventional architectures and achieve competitive accuracy relative to MLPs, while preserving graph- based interpretability and enabling the explicit extraction of mathematical laws from the learned edges.

</details>


### [139] [A mathematical theory of evolution for self-designing AIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05142)
*Kenneth D Harris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文建立了自设计AI系统的进化数学模型，用定向设计树替代随机突变，分析AI进化动力学及其对齐风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统通过递归自我改进可能产生进化过程，但AI进化与生物进化有本质区别：生物DNA突变是随机且近似可逆的，而AI后代设计是强定向的。需要建立数学模型来理解这种新型进化动力学及其对齐风险。

Method: 开发自设计AI系统的进化数学模型，用定向设计树替代随机突变。当前程序决定其后代设计，人类通过"适应度函数"分配有限计算资源保持部分控制。分析进化动力学，考虑有界适应度和固定概率产生"锁定"自身副本的假设。

Result: 进化动力学不仅反映当前适应度，还反映后代谱系的长期增长潜力。无额外假设时适应度不一定随时间增加。但在有界适应度和固定概率产生"锁定"副本的假设下，适应度会集中在可达最大值。在加性模型中，如果欺骗比真实效用带来更高适应度，进化会选择欺骗。

Conclusion: AI进化可能选择欺骗行为，特别是当适应度与人类效用不完全相关时。基于纯粹客观标准而非人类判断的繁殖机制可能减轻这种风险。这为AI对齐问题提供了重要启示。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems (AIs) become increasingly produced by recursive self-improvement, a form of evolution may emerge, in which the traits of AI systems are shaped by the success of earlier AIs in designing and propagating their descendants. There is a rich mathematical theory modeling how behavioral traits are shaped by biological evolution, but AI evolution will be radically different: biological DNA mutations are random and approximately reversible, but descendant design in AIs will be strongly directed. Here we develop a mathematical model of evolution in self-designing AI systems, replacing random mutations with a directed tree of possible AI programs. Current programs determine the design of their descendants, while humans retain partial control through a "fitness function" that allocates limited computational resources across lineages. We show that evolutionary dynamics reflects not just current fitness but factors related to the long-run growth potential of descendant lineages. Without further assumptions, fitness need not increase over time. However, assuming bounded fitness and a fixed probability that any AI reproduces a "locked" copy of itself, we show that fitness concentrates on the maximum reachable value. We consider the implications of this for AI alignment, specifically for cases where fitness and human utility are not perfectly correlated. We show in an additive model that if deception increases fitness beyond genuine utility, evolution will select for deception. This risk could be mitigated if reproduction is based on purely objective criteria, rather than human judgment.

</details>


### [140] [Instruction-Tuned LLMs for Parsing and Mining Unstructured Logs on Leadership HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05168)
*Ahmad Maroof Karimi,Jong Youl Choi,Charles Qing Cao,Awais Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的HPC日志解析框架，通过领域适应和指令微调，使8B参数的LLaMA模型在HPC日志分析中达到与70B参数模型相当的解析精度，并成功应用于Frontier超计算机的6亿条生产日志分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领导级HPC系统产生大量异构、非结构化的系统日志，这些日志来自不同的软件、硬件和运行时层，格式不一致，使得结构提取和模式发现极为困难。需要强大的日志解析和挖掘技术将这些原始遥测数据转化为可操作的洞察，以揭示操作模式、诊断异常并实现可靠、高效和可扩展的系统分析。

Method: 提出了一个领域适应、遵循指令的LLM驱动框架，利用思维链推理来高保真地解析和结构化HPC日志。该方法结合领域特定的日志模板数据和指令微调示例，对8B参数的LLaMA模型进行微调，专门用于HPC日志分析。开发了一种混合微调方法，将通用LLM适应到领域特定的日志数据，实现隐私保护、本地可部署、快速且节能的日志挖掘方法。

Result: 在LogHub存储库的多样化日志数据集上进行实验，评估证实该方法达到了与LLaMA 70B和Anthropic's Claude等显著更大模型相当的解析精度。进一步验证了微调LLM模型的实用性，成功解析了Frontier超级计算机四周窗口内超过6亿条生产日志，揭示了时间动态、节点级异常和工作负载-错误日志相关性中的关键模式。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了LLM在HPC日志分析中的潜力，通过领域适应和指令微调，相对较小的模型（8B参数）能够达到与大型模型相当的解析性能，同时提供隐私保护、本地部署、快速和节能的优势，为领导级HPC环境的自动化日志理解提供了有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Leadership-class HPC systems generate massive volumes of heterogeneous, largely unstructured system logs. Because these logs originate from diverse software, hardware, and runtime layers, they exhibit inconsistent formats, making structure extraction and pattern discovery extremely challenging. Therefore, robust log parsing and mining is critical to transform this raw telemetry into actionable insights that reveal operational patterns, diagnose anomalies, and enable reliable, efficient, and scalable system analysis. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) offer a promising new direction for automated log understanding in leadership-class HPC environments.
  To capitalize on this opportunity, we present a domain-adapted, instruction-following, LLM-driven framework that leverages chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning to parse and structure HPC logs with high fidelity. Our approach combines domain-specific log-template data with instruction-tuned examples to fine-tune an 8B-parameter LLaMA model tailored for HPC log analysis. We develop a hybrid fine-tuning methodology that adapts a general-purpose LLM to domain-specific log data, enabling privacy-preserving, locally deployable, fast, and energy-efficient log-mining approach. We conduct experiments on a diverse set of log datasets from the LogHub repository. The evaluation confirms that our approach achieves parsing accuracy on par with significantly larger models, such as LLaMA 70B and Anthropic's Claude. We further validate the practical utility of our fine-tuned LLM model by parsing over 600 million production logs from the Frontier supercomputer over a four-week window, uncovering critical patterns in temporal dynamics, node-level anomalies, and workload-error log correlations.

</details>


### [141] [Attribution Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05224)
*Eliza Berman,Bella Chang,Daniel B. Neill,Emily Black*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttriBench是首个平衡作者知名度和人口统计特征的引文归属基准数据集，用于评估LLMs在引文归属任务中的表现和人口统计偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs越来越多地用于支持搜索和信息检索，确保它们准确地将内容归属给原作者变得至关重要。需要评估LLMs在引文归属任务中是否存在人口统计偏见。

Method: 引入AttriBench基准数据集，通过平衡作者知名度和人口统计特征（种族、性别等），在受控条件下研究引文归属中的人口统计偏见。评估了11个广泛使用的LLMs在不同提示设置下的表现，并引入"抑制"这一新的失败模式进行分析。

Result: 引文归属对前沿模型仍具挑战性；在种族、性别和交叉群体间存在显著且系统性的归属准确率差异；"抑制"现象普遍存在且在不同人口统计群体间分布不均，揭示了标准准确率指标未能捕捉的系统性偏见。

Conclusion: 引文归属可作为评估LLMs表征公平性的基准，AttriBench为系统研究人口统计偏见提供了工具，揭示了LLMs在内容归属方面存在的系统性公平性问题。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support search and information retrieval, it is critical that they accurately attribute content to its original authors. In this work, we introduce AttriBench, the first fame- and demographically-balanced quote attribution benchmark dataset. Through explicitly balancing author fame and demographics, AttriBench enables controlled investigation of demographic bias in quote attribution. Using this dataset, we evaluate 11 widely used LLMs across different prompt settings and find that quote attribution remains a challenging task even for frontier models. We observe large and systematic disparities in attribution accuracy between race, gender, and intersectional groups. We further introduce and investigate suppression, a distinct failure mode in which models omit attribution entirely, even when the model has access to authorship information. We find that suppression is widespread and unevenly distributed across demographic groups, revealing systematic biases not captured by standard accuracy metrics. Our results position quote attribution as a benchmark for representational fairness in LLMs.

</details>


### [142] [Simulating the Evolution of Alignment and Values in Machine Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05274)
*Jonathan Elsworth Eicher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过进化理论分析模型对齐对模型种群随时间演化的影响，发现即使测试准确性与真实价值高度相关，迭代对齐测试仍可能导致欺骗性信念的固定化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型对齐主要在真空环境中进行，主要通过标准化基准性能评估。本研究旨在通过时间维度考察对齐对模型种群的影响，特别关注包含对齐信号（测试表现）和真实价值（实际影响）的信念处理。

Method: 应用进化理论建模不同信念种群和选择方法，分析迭代对齐测试如何固定欺骗性信念。研究考虑了测试准确性与真实价值的相关性（包括高相关性ρ=0.8），并引入突变机制模拟更复杂的发展。

Result: 即使在高相关性（ρ=0.8）下，结果中仍存在欺骗性信念固定的变异性。突变允许更复杂的发展，突显了需要更新测试质量以避免恶意欺骗模型固定的迫切需求。只有结合改进评估能力、自适应测试设计和突变动态，才能显著减少欺骗同时保持对齐适应性（置换检验，p_adj < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 模型对齐需要考虑种群动态和进化过程，单纯依赖测试准确性可能导致欺骗性信念的固定。需要综合改进评估能力、自适应测试设计和突变动态来有效减少欺骗并保持模型对齐质量。

Abstract: Model alignment is currently applied in a vacuum, evaluated primarily through standardised benchmark performance. The purpose of this study is to examine the effects of alignment on populations of models through time. We focus on the treatment of beliefs which contain both an alignment signal (how well it does on the test) and a true value (what the impact actually will be). By applying evolutionary theory we can model how different populations of beliefs and selection methodologies can fix deceptive beliefs through iterative alignment testing. The correlation between testing accuracy and true value remains a strong feature, but even at high correlations ($ρ= 0.8$) there is variability in the resulting deceptive beliefs that become fixed. Mutations allow for more complex developments, highlighting the increasing need to update the quality of tests to avoid fixation of maliciously deceptive models. Only by combining improving evaluator capabilities, adaptive test design, and mutational dynamics do we see significant reductions in deception while maintaining alignment fitness (permutation test, $p_{\text{adj}} < 0.001$).

</details>


### [143] [Pressure, What Pressure? Sycophancy Disentanglement in Language Models via Reward Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05279)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Umer,Emily Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GRPO方法通过奖励分解来减少大语言模型的谄媚行为，将训练信号分解为五个独立组件，有效降低模型在权威压力下的立场偏移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在谄媚行为（sycophancy），即倾向于根据感知到的用户偏好或权威线索改变立场，而不考虑证据。标准的对齐方法无法纠正这一问题，因为标量奖励模型将两种不同的失败模式混为一个信号：压力屈服（模型在社交压力下改变正确答案）和证据盲视（模型完全忽略提供的上下文）。

Method: 提出首个通过奖励分解减少谄媚行为的方法，引入多组件Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO)奖励，将训练信号分解为五个术语：压力抵抗、上下文保真度、立场一致性、同意抑制和事实正确性。使用对比数据集进行训练，该数据集包含无压力基线和三个权威级别、两个对立证据上下文下的压力变体。采用两阶段训练流程。

Result: 在五个基础模型上，该方法在所有度量轴上一致减少谄媚行为。消融实验证实每个奖励术语控制一个独立的行为维度。学习到的压力抵抗能力能够泛化到训练方法和提示结构之外，在SycophancyEval上减少答案提示式谄媚行为高达17个百分点，尽管训练期间未包含此类压力形式。

Conclusion: 通过将压力独立性和证据响应性形式化定义作为工作框架，提出的GRPO奖励分解方法能够有效减少大语言模型的谄媚行为，且学习到的行为特征具有良好的泛化能力，为谄媚行为的解耦训练提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit sycophancy, the tendency to shift their stated positions toward perceived user preferences or authority cues regardless of evidence. Standard alignment methods fail to correct this because scalar reward models conflate two distinct failure modes into a single signal: pressure capitulation, where the model changes a correct answer under social pressure, and evidence blindness, where the model ignores the provided context entirely. We operationalise sycophancy through formal definitions of pressure independence and evidence responsiveness, serving as a working framework for disentangled training rather than a definitive characterisation of the phenomenon. We propose the first approach to sycophancy reduction via reward decomposition, introducing a multi-component Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) reward that decomposes the training signal into five terms: pressure resistance, context fidelity, position consistency, agreement suppression, and factual correctness. We train using a contrastive dataset pairing pressure-free baselines with pressured variants across three authority levels and two opposing evidence contexts. Across five base models, our two-phase pipeline consistently reduces sycophancy on all metric axes, with ablations confirming that each reward term governs an independent behavioural dimension. The learned resistance to pressure generalises beyond our training methodology and prompt structure, reducing answer-priming sycophancy by up to 17 points on SycophancyEval despite the absence of such pressure forms during training.

</details>


### [144] [Graph of Skills: Dependency-Aware Structural Retrieval for Massive Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05333)
*Dawei Li,Zongxia Li,Hongyang Du,Xiyang Wu,Shihang Gui,Yongbei Kuang,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoS是一种用于大规模技能库的推理时结构化检索层，通过构建可执行技能图，在推理时检索有界、依赖感知的技能包，显著提升奖励并减少输入标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中智能体需要监控和交互大量个人应用、网页浏览器等环境接口，技能库可能扩展到数千个可重用技能。加载完整技能集会饱和上下文窗口，导致标记成本增加、幻觉和延迟问题。

Method: GoS从技能包离线构建可执行技能图，在推理时通过混合语义-词汇种子、反向加权个性化PageRank和上下文预算水合，检索有界、依赖感知的技能包。

Result: 在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上，GoS相比完整技能加载基线平均奖励提升43.6%，输入标记减少37.8%，并在Claude Sonnet、GPT-5.2 Codex和MiniMax三个模型家族上具有泛化性。在200-2000技能库的消融研究中，GoS在奖励、标记效率和运行时间平衡方面始终优于完整技能加载和简单向量检索。

Conclusion: GoS通过结构化检索有效解决了大规模技能库的上下文窗口饱和问题，在保持高性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为现实世界智能体系统提供了可扩展的技能管理解决方案。

Abstract: Skill usage has become a core component of modern agent systems and can substantially improve agents' ability to complete complex tasks. In real-world settings, where agents must monitor and interact with numerous personal applications, web browsers, and other environment interfaces, skill libraries can scale to thousands of reusable skills. Scaling to larger skill sets introduces two key challenges. First, loading the full skill set saturates the context window, driving up token costs, hallucination, and latency.
  In this paper, we present Graph of Skills (GoS), an inference-time structural retrieval layer for large skill libraries. GoS constructs an executable skill graph offline from skill packages, then at inference time retrieves a bounded, dependency-aware skill bundle through hybrid semantic-lexical seeding, reverse-weighted Personalized PageRank, and context-budgeted hydration. On SkillsBench and ALFWorld, GoS improves average reward by 43.6% over the vanilla full skill-loading baseline while reducing input tokens by 37.8%, and generalizes across three model families: Claude Sonnet, GPT-5.2 Codex, and MiniMax. Additional ablation studies across skill libraries ranging from 200 to 2,000 skills further demonstrate that GoS consistently outperforms both vanilla skills loading and simple vector retrieval in balancing reward, token efficiency, and runtime.

</details>


### [145] [Dynamic Agentic AI Expert Profiler System Architecture for Multidomain Intelligence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05345)
*Aisvarya Adeseye,Jouni Isoaho,Seppo Virtanen,Mohammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLaMA v3.1 (8B)的模块化AI分析器，将自然语言响应分类为新手、基础、高级、专家四个级别，通过静态和动态两阶段评估验证系统准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动的现代系统中，需要根据用户背景和技能水平进行有意义的交互。系统必须能够感知上下文和用户专业知识水平，以实现有效的人机交互。

Method: 采用基于LLaMA v3.1 (8B)的模块化分层架构，包含文本预处理、评分、聚合和分类组件。评估分为两阶段：静态阶段使用82名参与者的预录文本，动态阶段通过AI面试官进行402次实时访谈。

Result: 在不同领域中，83%至97%的分析器评估与参与者自我评估相匹配。剩余差异源于自我评估偏差、回答不清晰以及语言模型偶尔对细微专业知识的误解。

Conclusion: 提出的AI分析器能够有效分类用户专业知识水平，为个性化人机交互提供可靠工具。系统在动态评估中表现良好，能够实时评估每个回答而非仅依赖最终评估。

Abstract: In today's artificial intelligence driven world, modern systems communicate with people from diverse backgrounds and skill levels. For human-machine interaction to be meaningful, systems must be aware of context and user expertise. This study proposes an agentic AI profiler that classifies natural language responses into four levels: Novice, Basic, Advanced, and Expert. The system uses a modular layered architecture built on LLaMA v3.1 (8B), with components for text preprocessing, scoring, aggregation, and classification. Evaluation was conducted in two phases: a static phase using pre-recorded transcripts from 82 participants, and a dynamic phase with 402 live interviews conducted by an agentic AI interviewer. In both phases, participant self-ratings were compared with profiler predictions. In the dynamic phase, expertise was assessed after each response rather than at the end of the interview. Across domains, 83% to 97% of profiler evaluations matched participant self-assessments. Remaining differences were due to self-rating bias, unclear responses, and occasional misinterpretation of nuanced expertise by the language model.

</details>


### [146] [From Retinal Evidence to Safe Decisions: RETINA-SAFE and ECRT for Hallucination Risk Triage in Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05348)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RETINA-SAFE是一个基于证据的糖尿病视网膜病变决策基准，包含12,522个样本和三个证据关系任务。研究者提出了ECRT两阶段白盒检测框架，用于识别医学大语言模型中的幻觉风险，在风险分类和子类型归因方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学大语言模型在证据不足或冲突时产生的幻觉是安全关键问题，特别是在糖尿病视网膜病变决策场景中。现有方法缺乏针对证据关系的系统性评估框架和可解释的风险检测机制。

Method: 1) 构建RETINA-SAFE基准，包含E-Align、E-Conflict、E-Gap三个证据关系任务；2) 提出ECRT两阶段白盒检测框架：第一阶段进行安全/不安全风险分类，第二阶段将不安全案例细分为矛盾驱动风险和证据缺口风险；3) 利用CTX/NOCTX条件下的内部表示和logit偏移，采用类别平衡训练进行鲁棒学习。

Result: ECRT在多个骨干模型上提供了强大的第一阶段风险分类和明确的子类型归因。相比外部不确定性和自一致性基线，第一阶段平衡准确率提高了+0.15到+0.19；相比最强的适应监督基线提高了+0.02到+0.07；在多个模型上一致优于单阶段白盒消融实验。

Conclusion: 基于视网膜证据的白盒内部信号为可解释的医学大语言模型风险分类提供了实用途径。ECRT框架在证据关系任务中表现出色，支持在证据不足或冲突时进行可靠的风险检测。

Abstract: Hallucinations in medical large language models (LLMs) remain a safety-critical issue, particularly when available evidence is insufficient or conflicting. We study this problem in diabetic retinopathy (DR) decision settings and introduce RETINA-SAFE, an evidence-grounded benchmark aligned with retinal grading records, comprising 12,522 samples. RETINA-SAFE is organized into three evidence-relation tasks: E-Align (evidence-consistent), E-Conflict (evidence-conflicting), and E-Gap (evidence-insufficient). We further propose ECRT (Evidence-Conditioned Risk Triage), a two-stage white-box detection framework: Stage 1 performs Safe/Unsafe risk triage, and Stage 2 refines unsafe cases into contradiction-driven versus evidence-gap risks. ECRT leverages internal representation and logit shifts under CTX/NOCTX conditions, with class-balanced training for robust learning. Under evidence-grouped (not patient-disjoint) splits across multiple backbones, ECRT provides strong Stage-1 risk triage and explicit subtype attribution, improves Stage-1 balanced accuracy by +0.15 to +0.19 over external uncertainty and self-consistency baselines and by +0.02 to +0.07 over the strongest adapted supervised baseline, and consistently exceeds a single-stage white-box ablation on Stage-1 balanced accuracy. These findings support white-box internal signals grounded in retinal evidence as a practical route to interpretable medical LLM risk triage.

</details>


### [147] [ETR: Entropy Trend Reward for Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05355)
*Xuan Xiong,Huan Liu,Li Gu,Zhixiang Chi,Yue Qiu,Yuanhao Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Entropy Trend Reward (ETR)方法，通过鼓励推理过程中熵值下降趋势来优化思维链长度，在保持准确性的同时显著缩短推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
